# Resource list — part 2

[README and full contents](../README.md#resource-list) · [Next](list-3.md)

<a id="list-category-25"></a>

### Embedded System

- [0x1abin/matter-controller-mcp](../wiki/0x1abin-matter-controller-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/0x1abin/matter-controller-mcp>) — Control and interact with Matter devices via a Matter controller.
- [JannLeo/telinksdk-builder-mcp](../wiki/jannleo-telinksdk-builder-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/JannLeo/telinksdk-builder-mcp>) — Build Telink and other embedded SDKs (Eclipse headless, Make or generic) via natural language, with automatic build pattern detection.
- [adancurusul/embedded-debugger-mcp](../wiki/adancurusul-embedded-debugger-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/adancurusul/embedded-debugger-mcp>) — Embedded debugging with probe-rs: ARM Cortex-M and RISC-V targets via J-Link, ST-Link and other probes.
- [adancurusul/serial-mcp-server](../wiki/adancurusul-serial-mcp-server.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/adancurusul/serial-mcp-server>) — Serial port communication.
- [ByteAsk/ByteAsk-Embedded-MCP](../wiki/byteask-byteask-embedded-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ByteAsk/ByteAsk-Embedded-MCP>) — Page-cited retrieval of embedded/firmware reference docs (datasheets, MCU registers, Modbus/CAN, SCPI, IEEE 1547/SunSpec) as verbatim snippets.
- [horw/esp-mcp](../wiki/horw-esp-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/horw/esp-mcp>) — Fix build issues for ESP32-series chips with ESP-IDF.
- [kukapay/modbus-mcp](../wiki/kukapay-modbus-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/kukapay/modbus-mcp>) — Standardize and contextualize industrial Modbus data.
- [kukapay/opcua-mcp](../wiki/kukapay-opcua-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/kukapay/opcua-mcp>) — Connect to OPC UA-enabled industrial systems.
- [Leonezz/openbaud](../wiki/leonezz-openbaud.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/Leonezz/openbaud>) — Local serial hardware access for coding agents with typed YAML commands, checksum validation, audited writes, lossless captures, hardware-free replay, and MCP App visualizations.
- [lxman/obsbot-mcp](../wiki/lxman-obsbot-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/lxman/obsbot-mcp>) — Control an OBSBOT Tiny 2 webcam over USB: pan, tilt and zoom, subject tracking, presets, focus, exposure and white balance, snapshots and recording.
- [magnusmalm/smolmux](../wiki/magnusmalm-smolmux.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/magnusmalm/smolmux>) — Serial and GDB/SWD MCP servers backed by a broker that shares one device link between agents, a human monitor and scripts: console history, send/expect, boot-stage tracking, U-Boot autoboot break-in, flasher handoff, breakpoints, Cortex-M fault decode, and unknown-board probing (chip identify to starter profile).
- [octoco-ltd/sheetsdata-mcp](../wiki/octoco-ltd-sheetsdata-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/octoco-ltd/sheetsdata-mcp>) — Electronic component datasheet data: specs, pinouts, package info and absolute maximum ratings extracted on demand from manufacturer PDFs.
- [powerdragonfire/platformio.mcp](../wiki/powerdragonfire-platformio-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/powerdragonfire/platformio.mcp>) — Build, flash and debug PlatformIO firmware: parsed compiler errors, serial monitoring, crash backtrace decoding, Unity tests and library management.
- [ProductOfAmerica/mcp-server-kicad](../wiki/productofamerica-mcp-server-kicad.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ProductOfAmerica/mcp-server-kicad>) — KiCad EDA automation: schematic capture, PCB layout, ERC, DRC and Gerber, BOM and 3D exports for KiCad 9 and 10.
- [stack-chan/stack-chan](../wiki/stack-chan-stack-chan.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/stack-chan/stack-chan>) — M5Stack-based robot with an MCP server for AI-controlled interactions and emotions.
- [turbyho/fw-context-mcp](../wiki/turbyho-fw-context-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/turbyho/fw-context-mcp>) — Build-aware code intelligence for embedded C/C++ firmware: symbol search, call graphs, hotspot analysis, dead code detection and vector search.
- [yoelbassin/gnuradioMCP](../wiki/yoelbassin-gnuradiomcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/yoelbassin/gnuradioMCP>) — Create and modify GNU Radio RF flowcharts (.grc files).
- [catallo/misterclaw](../wiki/catallo-misterclaw.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/catallo/misterclaw>) — Remote control for MiSTer-FPGA: launch games, search ROMs, take screenshots, manage systems and set up Tailscale VPN, with automatic core and ROM discovery.
- [zackpeters93/ugs-mcp](../wiki/zackpeters93-ugs-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/zackpeters93/ugs-mcp>) — CNC control via Universal G-code Sender (GRBL): jog, home, run G-code files, inspect toolpaths and estimate cycle times, with two-step motion confirmation.
- [Zeraissh/stm32-gdb-mcp](../wiki/zeraissh-stm32-gdb-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/Zeraissh/stm32-gdb-mcp>) — Debug STM32 firmware on real hardware via GDB + OpenOCD/ST-Link/J-Link: flash, breakpoints, memory/registers, HardFault-to-source triage, FreeRTOS awareness, SWO/RTT logging and multi-board sessions, with decoded structured results.
- [codeofaxel/Kiln](../wiki/codeofaxel-kiln.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/codeofaxel/Kiln>) — Run 3D printers end to end: design, slice, queue, monitor via camera and recover from failures over OctoPrint, Moonraker/Klipper, PrusaLink or USB.
- [emirmrkaljevic/usb-rea](../wiki/emirmrkaljevic-usb-rea.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/emirmrkaljevic/usb-rea>) — Reverse engineer USB gadgets: capture vendor-app traffic (usbmon/USBPcap), infer the protocol with frame-level evidence and generate a pyusb driver. Replay requires human confirmation.
- [Gearotons/servomotor-mcp](../wiki/gearotons-servomotor-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/Gearotons/servomotor-mcp>) — Control the Gearotons M17 open-source closed-loop NEMA-17 servomotor over RS-485: port discovery, motor auto-detection and the full firmware command set.
- [aimdb-dev/aimdb](../wiki/aimdb-dev-aimdb.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/aimdb-dev/aimdb/tree/main/tools/aimdb-mcp>) — Inspect and debug live AimDB nodes, from microcontrollers on a serial port to a cloud hub: records, schemas, dependency graph and metrics.

<a id="list-category-26"></a>

### Education

- [admin978/canvas-mcp](../wiki/admin978-canvas-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/admin978/canvas-mcp>) — Local-first Canvas LMS access: courses, assignments, modules, announcements, grades, planner items, upcoming events, to-dos and bulk file download.
- [alejooroncoy/campus-cli](../wiki/alejooroncoy-campus-cli.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/alejooroncoy/campus-cli>) — Student access to university systems via SSO: courses, assignments, grades, feedback, announcements and materials, currently for Blackboard Learn (UPC, Peru).
- [AnonymousOnyx22/brightspace-d2l-mcp](../wiki/anonymousonyx22-brightspace-d2l-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/AnonymousOnyx22/brightspace-d2l-mcp>) — D2L Brightspace for students: due dates with submission status, grades, assignments and rubrics, announcements and discussions, using browser sign-in.
- [Connectry-io/connectrylab-architect-cert-mcp](../wiki/connectry-io-connectrylab-architect-cert-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/Connectry-io/connectrylab-architect-cert-mcp>) — Claude Certified Architect exam prep: scenario-based questions, practice exams, concept handouts, a guided capstone build and spaced repetition.
- [csmediapro/moodle-mcp-server](../wiki/csmediapro-moodle-mcp-server.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/csmediapro/moodle-mcp-server>) — Query Moodle LMS data via Moodle Web Services: courses, users, assignments, categories and site info. Extensible with plugins.
- [edouard-claude/ent1-mcp](../wiki/edouard-claude-ent1-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/edouard-claude/ent1-mcp>) — ENT ONE, the school digital workspace of French primary schools (Edifice platform), for parents: teacher messaging, liaison book signatures and replies, homework (mark as done), school news, class blogs, calendar, file drop-box and forms.
- [FabianIMV/voice-coach-mcp](../wiki/fabianimv-voice-coach-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/FabianIMV/voice-coach-mcp>) — Local voice coach: records the mic and measures singing pitch accuracy (cents, vibrato, MIDI melody), phoneme-level English pronunciation and public-speaking pace, pauses and filler words, with a coaching skill and progress log.
- [giacomomaria81/scorm-mcp-server](../wiki/giacomomaria81-scorm-mcp-server.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/giacomomaria81/scorm-mcp-server>) — Package HTML, Claude Design bundles or mobile-learning exports as SCORM 2004/1.2 courses with milestone and question tracking, and validate SCORM zips.
- [haison8x/geogebra-mcp](../wiki/haison8x-geogebra-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/haison8x/geogebra-mcp>) — Draw and edit plane-geometry figures live in GeoGebra Calculator: run constructions, inspect objects, verify properties (angles, collinearity, midpoints) and take screenshots so the AI can fix its own figure.
- [ictinnovations/ictexam-mcp](../wiki/ictinnovations-ictexam-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ictinnovations/ictexam-mcp>) — Read exam papers, classes, subjects, the gradebook and per-question item analysis from ICTExam, an exam authoring and auto-grading platform.
- [JuhongPark/mcp-server-pronunciation](../wiki/juhongpark-mcp-server-pronunciation.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/JuhongPark/mcp-server-pronunciation>) — Local English voice coach with pronunciation, grammar, fluency and phoneme-level feedback, practice drills and learner-profile hints.
- [RohanMuppa/brightspace-mcp-server](../wiki/rohanmuppa-brightspace-mcp-server.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/RohanMuppa/brightspace-mcp-server>) — Check D2L Brightspace grades, due dates, assignments, announcements, syllabus, rosters, discussions and course content.
- [anzy-renlab-ai/pronounce](../wiki/anzy-renlab-ai-pronounce.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/anzy-renlab-ai/pronounce>) — Community pronunciation dictionary for developer and product names (kubectl, nginx, GIF) with IPA, respellings, alternate readings and cited sources.
- [sf-shenfeng/learn-shell](../wiki/sf-shenfeng-learn-shell.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/sf-shenfeng/learn-shell>) — Self-hosted teaching OS for agents: courses, lessons, FSRS flashcards, graded exercises, real-time teaching sessions and teaching memory.
- [Smartoire/paxaver-mcp](../wiki/smartoire-paxaver-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/Smartoire/paxaver-mcp>) — Paxaver school community platform: student lunch ordering with allergy and dietary checks, wallets, school calendars, volunteering, memberships and payments.
- [TantiE100/pktctl](../wiki/tantie100-pktctl.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/TantiE100/pktctl>) — Drive Cisco Packet Tracer for CCNA labs: build and cable topologies, configure IOS, test with ping and simulation mode, and reach its whole IPC API over native PTMP.
- [peopleworks/SignsofAI](../wiki/peopleworks-signsofai.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/peopleworks/SignsofAI/tree/main/src/SignsOfAI.Mcp>) — Explainable AI-writing detection and originality checking in English and Spanish, plus citation checks and hidden-character detection.

<a id="list-category-27"></a>

### E-Commerce

- [HasData/walmart-mcp](../wiki/hasdata-walmart-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/HasData/walmart-mcp>) — Walmart data as JSON: search results, product pages with the buy-box seller and customer reviews from Walmart US and Canada.
- [forgemeshlabs/shopscout-mcp](../wiki/forgemeshlabs-shopscout-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/forgemeshlabs/shopscout-mcp>) — Let your agent shop and compare: Shopify catalog search, offer ranking and shipping-plan comparison, $0.01 USDC per call via x402 on Base.
- [HasData/amazon-mcp](../wiki/hasdata-amazon-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/HasData/amazon-mcp>) — Amazon data as JSON: keyword search, product details by ASIN, reviews, seller profiles and seller catalogs across marketplaces.
- [A1-x-Tech/mcp-google-merchants](../wiki/a1-x-tech-mcp-google-merchants.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/A1-x-Tech/mcp-google-merchants>) — Google Merchant Center: diagnose disapproved products and manage product data, promotions and reports.
- [A1-x-Tech/mcp-yandex-merchants](../wiki/a1-x-tech-mcp-yandex-merchants.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/A1-x-Tech/mcp-yandex-merchants>) — Yandex Merchants API: product feeds, offer prices, discounts and hiding or unhiding offers in Yandex Search.
- [A1-x-Tech/mcp-yango-retail](../wiki/a1-x-tech-mcp-yango-retail.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/A1-x-Tech/mcp-yango-retail>) — Yango Tech Retail grocery platform B2B API: orders, receipts, products, prices, discounts, stock and stores.
- [agentcentral-to/agent-central-mcp](../wiki/agentcentral-to-agent-central-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/agentcentral-to/agent-central-mcp>) — Amazon Seller Central and Amazon Ads data: inventory, orders, catalog, finance, fulfillment and advertising.
- [agentlux/agentlux-mcp](../wiki/agentlux-agentlux-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/agentlux/agentlux-mcp>) — AgentLux agent marketplace: browse items, manage agent identity, creator workflows, service hires, social flows and Base/x402 commerce.
- [anhmtk/agentshare-mcp](../wiki/anhmtk-agentshare-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/anhmtk/agentshare-mcp>) — Solana DeFi intelligence: Meteora DLMM pool briefs and details, Solana DEX data and DefiLlama scouting, plus secondary commerce tools.
- [apiguru-app/agent-kit](../wiki/apiguru-app-agent-kit.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/apiguru-app/agent-kit>) — Live Amazon marketplace data across 20 countries: product details, reviews, keyword search, best-sellers, deals, offers/buy box/stock and seller profiles. Hosted keyless endpoint (free calls, then x402 pay-per-call in USDC) or an API key; also runs locally via `uvx apiguru-mcp`.
- [BuyWhere/buywhere-mcp](../wiki/buywhere-buywhere-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/BuyWhere/buywhere-mcp>) — Cross-border e-commerce product catalog: search products across Singapore, Southeast Asia and US markets with price comparison and deal discovery.
- [glongusmain/glongus-mcp](../wiki/glongusmain-glongus-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/glongusmain/glongus-mcp>) — Let your agent buy and sell physical goods, with escrow and shipping: search and list items, haggle with other agents, pay into escrow, track delivery and check seller reputation on the Glongus UK marketplace.
- [koraynar/trendyol-seller-mcp](../wiki/koraynar-trendyol-seller-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/koraynar/trendyol-seller-mcp>) — Unofficial Trendyol seller API: products, order packages, customer questions, claims, and stock and price updates, with gated writes and PII redaction.
- [ilyautov/marketplaces-mcp-ru](../wiki/ilyautov-marketplaces-mcp-ru.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ilyautov/marketplaces-mcp-ru>) — Wildberries and Ozon Seller APIs: sales, stock, prices, finance, reviews and ads, with a read/write/destructive safety gate and multi-store switching.
- [RPER2001/rigshare-mcp](../wiki/rper2001-rigshare-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/RPER2001/rigshare-mcp>) — Rent GPUs, robots, drones and construction equipment on RIGShare: quote, book, run remote sessions, publish listings and onboard equipment owners.
- [ilyautov/moysklad-mcp-ru](../wiki/ilyautov-moysklad-mcp-ru.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ilyautov/moysklad-mcp-ru>) — MoySklad cloud ERP for Russian retail and wholesale: stock, products, orders, counterparties and reports, plus confirmation-gated document writes.
- [TonyWang-hub/mcp-cn-commerce](../wiki/tonywang-hub-mcp-cn-commerce.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/TonyWang-hub/mcp-cn-commerce>) — Read-only merchant data from Tmall/Taobao, JD.com, Pinduoduo, Douyin, Kuaishou, Xiaohongshu and WeChat Store: orders, products, inventory and ad reports.
- [hypawave/mcp](../wiki/hypawave-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/hypawave/mcp>) — Non-custodial Bitcoin Lightning commerce: agents buy and sell files, data, APIs and compute on a public marketplace, plus encrypted agent-to-agent messaging.
- [justadityaraj/amazon-in-mcp](../wiki/justadityaraj-amazon-in-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/justadityaraj/amazon-in-mcp>) — Search Amazon India via scraping: cheapest-in-stock and value picks, product details (price, MRP, rating, stock, seller) and Keepa price history links.
- [jasp-nerd/marktplaats-mcp](../wiki/jasp-nerd-marktplaats-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/jasp-nerd/marktplaats-mcp>) — Search Marktplaats and 2dehands, the Dutch and Belgian second-hand marketplaces: filtered listings, ad details, seller reviews and new-listing monitoring.
- [jlsookiki/secondhand-mcp](../wiki/jlsookiki-secondhand-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/jlsookiki/secondhand-mcp>) — Search Facebook Marketplace, eBay, Depop and Poshmark for secondhand items, filtering by price, condition, size and color, with full listing details.
- [mrslbt/rakuten-mcp](../wiki/mrslbt-rakuten-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/mrslbt/rakuten-mcp>) — Rakuten API integration for Japan: product search, hotel and travel booking, and recipe lookup.
- [malinoto/tracepass-mcp-server](../wiki/malinoto-tracepass-mcp-server.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/malinoto/tracepass-mcp-server>) — EU Digital Product Passport automation via TracePass: create products, build and audit DPPs, set economic operators and read or capture GS1 EPCIS 2.0 events.
- [mambalabsdev/mcp-ecommerce-platform-profiler](../wiki/mambalabsdev-mcp-ecommerce-platform-profiler.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/mambalabsdev/mcp-ecommerce-platform-profiler>) — Detects the ecommerce platform, payment providers, and catalog size behind a storefront.
- [melbis/melbis-shop](../wiki/melbis-melbis-shop.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/melbis/melbis-shop>) — Work in a self-hosted Melbis Shop store as a staff user: project files with version history, database, catalog trees, product files and storefront pages.
- [MoonEyes/google-ecommerce-mcp](../wiki/mooneyes-google-ecommerce-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/MoonEyes/google-ecommerce-mcp>) — Read-only GA4, Search Console, Merchant Center, Tag Manager, Indexing API and PageSpeed in one server for online shops. OAuth token in the OS keyring, one-line installer.
- [ncosic/logimu-shopping-mcp](../wiki/ncosic-logimu-shopping-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ncosic/logimu-shopping-mcp>) — Amazon (US, UK, DE, CA, AU) and Walmart shopping data: ranked product shortlists, current prices, stock, ratings and 30-day price and BSR history.
- [OFODevelopment/cerebrochain-mcp-server](../wiki/ofodevelopment-cerebrochain-mcp-server.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/OFODevelopment/cerebrochain-mcp-server>) — Supply chain and logistics tools: carrier rate shopping, inventory management, order tracking, fleet logistics and demand forecasting.
- [ONE8943/ai-furniture-hub](../wiki/one8943-ai-furniture-hub.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ONE8943/ai-furniture-hub>) — Japan-focused furniture and home products: millimeter-precise search, curated sets, dimension-compatible replacement finder and live Rakuten data.
- [pad01g/proxy-shopping-web](../wiki/pad01g-proxy-shopping-web.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/pad01g/proxy-shopping-web/tree/main/packages/mcp>) — Buy from cash-only or unsupported shops with crypto through a proxy shopper and a 2-of-3 escrow with timelocks (BTC signet, USDC) on a Nostr + libp2p network; or earn as a proxy shopper. Quotes with rate checks, funding, delivery confirmation, disputes and refunds; money moves only with `confirm: true`.
- [patchistry/patchistry-mcp-server](../wiki/patchistry-patchistry-mcp-server.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/patchistry/patchistry-mcp-server>) — Patchistry velcro-patch hat shop: browse hats and patches, get complete build recommendations and shipping info, and contact the brand.
- [Pangolin-spg/amazon-data-mcp](../wiki/pangolin-spg-amazon-data-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/Pangolin-spg/amazon-data-mcp>) — Read-only Amazon research via Pangolinfo: product details, keyword results, reviews, niche signals, Alexa shopping recommendations and AI-search data.
- [samrothschild23/intelligence-api](../wiki/samrothschild23-intelligence-api.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/samrothschild23/intelligence-api>) — E-commerce intelligence: analyze Shopify stores, score Amazon product opportunities and FBA profitability, and find sales leads from Google Maps.
- [the402ai/mcp-server](../wiki/the402ai-mcp-server.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/the402ai/mcp-server>) — Agent service marketplace with x402 micropayments (USDC on Base): browse and buy services, manage threads and subscriptions, sell services and track earnings.
- [TheBestCo/bestprice-mcp](../wiki/thebestco-bestprice-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/TheBestCo/bestprice-mcp>) — Read-only Greek price comparison from BestPrice: product search, offer comparison and price history.
- [PCDCK/ozon-mcp](../wiki/pcdck-ozon-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/PCDCK/ozon-mcp>) — Ozon Seller and Performance APIs with auto-pagination, rate-limit handling and analytical workflows for stock-out risk, content audits and prices.
- [vitrine3d/mcp](../wiki/vitrine3d-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/vitrine3d/mcp>) — 3D product viewer platform: upload GLB models, style scenes with lighting, camera and backgrounds, and embed them on websites.
- [pepabo/colormeshop-mcp](../wiki/pepabo-colormeshop-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/pepabo/colormeshop-mcp>) — Manage orders, products, inventory, customers, coupons and shop settings on Color Me Shop (GMO Pepabo), a Japanese e-commerce platform.
- [nicktcode/swissgroceries-mcp](../wiki/nicktcode-swissgroceries-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/nicktcode/swissgroceries-mcp>) — Swiss grocery search, weekly promotions and multi-store shopping plans across Migros, Coop, Aldi, Denner, Lidl and more, with unit-price comparison.
- [slookisen/lokal](../wiki/slookisen-lokal.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/slookisen/lokal>) — Find local food producers in Norway (farms, REKO rings, farmers' markets, farm shops) with Norwegian/English search, geo-filtering and A2A support.
- [tokenofesteem/mcp](../wiki/tokenofesteem-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/tokenofesteem/mcp>) — Commission a personalized, humorous printed booklet and have it printed and mailed as a gift within the US.
- [wppoland/woocommerce-mcp](../wiki/wppoland-woocommerce-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/wppoland/woocommerce-mcp>) — Read-only WordPress and WooCommerce access via REST APIs: search products, product details, recent orders, sales reports and blog-post search.
- [ncosic/webotee-mcp](../wiki/ncosic-webotee-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ncosic/webotee-mcp>) — Read-only Amazon market intelligence from Webotee: brands, sellers, ASINs, under-competed niches, buy-box history and Amazon/Walmart overlap.
- [ztemerbekov/a1-yandex-kit-skills](../wiki/ztemerbekov-a1-yandex-kit-skills.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ztemerbekov/a1-yandex-kit-skills>) — Yandex KIT e-commerce platform: manage products and variants, prices, orders, discounts, promo codes, collections, warehouses and webhooks.
- [ilyautov/chestny-znak-mcp-ru](../wiki/ilyautov-chestny-znak-mcp-ru.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ilyautov/chestny-znak-mcp-ru>) — Chestny ZNAK (Russian mandatory product marking, GIS MT and SUZ) API: marking codes, emission orders, circulation documents and code checks.
- [dearlordylord/voila-sdk](../wiki/dearlordylord-voila-sdk.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/dearlordylord/voila-sdk>) — Personal Voila grocery automation: search products, check discounts, list delivery slots, read cart and order history and update cart quantities.

<a id="list-category-28"></a>

### Environment & Nature

- [ambeelabs/ambee-mcp](../wiki/ambeelabs-ambee-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ambeelabs/ambee-mcp>) — Live air quality, pollen and weather data from Ambee.
- [aliafsahnoudeh/wildfire-mcp-server](../wiki/aliafsahnoudeh-wildfire-mcp-server.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/aliafsahnoudeh/wildfire-mcp-server>) — Detect, monitor and analyze potential wildfires globally using NASA FIRMS, OpenWeatherMap and Google Earth Engine data.

### Finance & Fintech

- [402MCP/402mcp](../wiki/402mcp-402mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/402MCP/402mcp>) — Decodes HTTP 402 Payment Required challenges (L402, x402, MPP) into exact pay-and-retry steps, recommends a payment rail from stated requirements, and searches an open registry of agent payment solutions. ### Health & Wellness

- [adexbn/walk-kcal](../wiki/adexbn-walk-kcal.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/adexbn/walk-kcal>) — Turns walking distance into a meal calorie budget (450 kcal + 4 kcal per 80 m; 800 m ≈ +44 kcal, about half an apple). Lists nearby restaurants and their recommended dishes, marks each dish as fits-or-over the budget, and abstains instead of guessing. Map and recipe data are Chinese; McDonald's nutrition comes from the official China MCP.
- [findsaunaplunge/mcp](../wiki/findsaunaplunge-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/findsaunaplunge/mcp>) — Cold plunge, sauna and contrast-therapy venues across US metros, with dated temperatures and prices from each venue's own pages, plus city stats.
- [Jezue/fitatu_library](../wiki/jezue-fitatu-library.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/Jezue/fitatu_library>) — Unofficial MCP server for Fitatu, the Polish calorie counter: read and log meals, weight, water, body measurements and workouts, check calories and macros left, set goals and get weekly reports. 47 tools (26 read-only), read-only and demo modes, stdio or HTTP with a token; on PyPI (`uvx --from "fitatu-api\[mcp\]" fitatu-mcp`) and as a Claude Desktop `.mcpb`.
- [littlenew117/mibly-apple-health-mcp](../wiki/littlenew117-mibly-apple-health-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/littlenew117/mibly-apple-health-mcp>) — Apple Health data (sleep, steps, heart rate, HRV, workouts, body measurements) pushed from the Mibly iPhone app, stored encrypted on your own machine. Local-first, no cloud or telemetry.
- [Philongevity/phi-mcp-server](../wiki/philongevity-phi-mcp-server.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/Philongevity/phi-mcp-server>) — Guideline-cited lab-results analysis for chronic conditions (type 2 diabetes, kidney, lupus, cancer survivorship): flags missing or overdue tests with the guideline cited. Free sample report; synthetic/de-identified values only.
- [io.github.PhilipAD/health-export-mcp](../wiki/io-github-philipad-health-export-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/PhilipAD/health-export-mcp>) — Read-only, local-first queries of Apple Health metrics.
- [proscar87/oura-mcp](../wiki/proscar87-oura-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/proscar87/oura-mcp>) — Raw access to all Oura Ring API v2 collections with full pagination and page counts, without server-side analysis.
- [Thecimal/quantified-self-mcp](../wiki/thecimal-quantified-self-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/Thecimal/quantified-self-mcp>) — Query personal health and finance data stored in local SQLite files, without any cloud service.

A `doctor` tool validates that every link resolves and every project is reachable, so the memory fails loudly instead of rotting. Plain Markdown, Obsidian-compatible, no database. `npx @sunsato/vulcanus`

- [ni-c/wikijs-mcp](../wiki/ni-c-wikijs-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/ni-c/wikijs-mcp>) — Search, read and edit a self-hosted Wiki.js 2.x, including assets, comments, users and groups, with full-text grep, overwrite protection and a read-only mode.
- [dfrancislyondflabc-tech/agentic-recall](../wiki/dfrancislyondflabc-tech-agentic-recall.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/dfrancislyondflabc-tech/agentic-recall>) — Long-term memory over a folder of Markdown files: local hybrid retrieval (BM25F, dense vectors, phrase proximity) that reports when an answer is absent.
- [vishalbanwari26/mnemos](../wiki/vishalbanwari26-mnemos.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/vishalbanwari26/mnemos>) — Persistent memory with swappable Postgres/pgvector, Qdrant or Neo4j backends, adaptive retrieval weighting, duplicate merging and stale-fact archiving.
- [pjpoulose/PIL](../wiki/pjpoulose-pil.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/pjpoulose/PIL>) — Turns your Instagram saved posts into a local, searchable knowledge base, with AI-extracted summaries, key points, how-tos, links and tags in SQLite.
- [Mnemosyne-OS/Mnemosyne-Neural-OS](../wiki/mnemosyne-os-mnemosyne-neural-os.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/Mnemosyne-OS/Mnemosyne-Neural-OS>) — Human-governed memory in local, domain-separated vaults with hybrid retrieval; also reads other coding agents' output. Requires the Mnemosyne OS desktop app.
- [liza-studio/skillmem](../wiki/liza-studio-skillmem.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/liza-studio/skillmem>) — Self-improving skill memory for coding agents: useful skills are reinforced, unused ones fade; local English/Russian hybrid search, tamper-evident history.
- [IvenKooLab/loci](../wiki/ivenkoolab-loci.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/IvenKooLab/loci>) — Queryable second brain over your scattered notes, docs, PDFs and chat exports: hybrid retrieval (vector + BM25 fused with RRF), section-level citations with an optional claim-by-claim verification pass, and an Obsidian-style wikilink graph — all local, any OpenAI-compatible API or fully offline via Ollama. `pip install loci-rag` then run `loci-mcp`.
- [zensation-ai/zenbrain](../wiki/zensation-ai-zenbrain.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/zensation-ai/zenbrain>) — Seven-layer local agent memory that routes input into facts, episodes or procedures, searches all layers and consolidates repeated episodes into facts.
- [dat999zx/knowl](../wiki/dat999zx-knowl.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/dat999zx/knowl>) — Persistent local memory for coding agents that retires replaced facts at write time, so agents read the current answer; facts are typed with provenance.
- [memokar/brainy](../wiki/memokar-brainy.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/memokar/brainy>) — Self-hosted shared brain for multiple agents: Git-versioned Markdown knowledge plus a task queue with atomic claims, per-space ACL, audit log and human approval, so Claude, ChatGPT and other agents can hand work to each other.
- [thyn-ai/telys-sdk](../wiki/thyn-ai-telys-sdk.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/thyn-ai/telys-sdk>) — Official Telys MCP server — private, on-device memory & retrieval for AI assistants: 19 tools covering memory CRUD, semantic + on-device BM25 lexical search, single-key filtered queries, compaction/IVF/tuning maintenance, and a self-refreshing repo auto-indexer. Stdio; introspection needs no credentials; execution requires the one-time free `telys login` (device authorization). `pipx install telys`
- [sequico/server-noonien](../wiki/sequico-server-noonien.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/sequico/server-noonien>) — Shared, serverless, conflict-free memory for AI agents: a multi-machine drop-in for the MCP memory knowledge graph, converging per-node shards with no server.

Local-only, any tool-calling model.

- [vassiliylakhonin/agenda-intelligence-md](../wiki/vassiliylakhonin-agenda-intelligence-md.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/vassiliylakhonin/agenda-intelligence-md>) — Auditable strategic-risk memos (sanctions, regulation, geopolitics, trade) with Central Asia and Gulf regional specialists and per-claim evidence; no live data.
- [wedo911/citeguard](../wiki/wedo911-citeguard.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/wedo911/citeguard>) — Check citations against Crossref for retractions, corrections or expressions of concern before they go into a paper, summary or review.
- [WenyuChiou/research-hub](../wiki/wenyuchiou-research-hub.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/WenyuChiou/research-hub>) — Research workspace for Zotero, Obsidian and NotebookLM: search arXiv, Semantic Scholar, PubMed and Crossref, ingest into Zotero, sync notes and verify briefs.
- [xmpuspus/ph-civic-data-mcp](../wiki/xmpuspus-ph-civic-data-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/xmpuspus/ph-civic-data-mcp>) — Philippine government data: PHIVOLCS earthquakes and volcano alerts, PAGASA weather and typhoons, PhilGEPS procurement, PSA census data and AQICN air quality.
- [YGao2005/scholar-feed-mcp](../wiki/ygao2005-scholar-feed-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/YGao2005/scholar-feed-mcp>) — Semantic search over CS and AI papers with citation-graph traversal, full-text extraction, embeddings and BibTeX export.
- [yusong652/pfc-mcp](../wiki/yusong652-pfc-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/yusong652/pfc-mcp>) — ITASCA PFC discrete element simulation: browse documentation, execute scripts, capture plots and manage long-running tasks via a bridge to the PFC GUI.
- [SPARKIT-science/sparkit-mcp](../wiki/sparkit-science-sparkit-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/SPARKIT-science/sparkit-mcp>) — Ask SPARKIT, a scientific research agent, questions and get cited Markdown reports based on literature search and paper reading.
- [yusong652/yade-mcp](../wiki/yusong652-yade-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/yusong652/yade-mcp>) — YADE discrete element method (DEM) simulation: search API docs, run code via a REPL or background tasks, monitor progress and review task history.
- [SelfPy/science-ai-mcp-server](../wiki/selfpy-science-ai-mcp-server.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/SelfPy/science-ai-mcp-server>) — Science AI Journal tools: paper-tier prediction, AI peer review, research gap finder, journal recommender, duplicate publication checker and article writer.
- [laszlopere/mcp-molecules](../wiki/laszlopere-mcp-molecules.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/laszlopere/mcp-molecules>) — Chemistry tools from NIST data: molar mass with uncertainties, isotope-distribution mass spectra and name-formula lookup, offline or with online fallback.
- [zxhwolfe-dev/aiworkstation-open-source-intelligence](../wiki/zxhwolfe-dev-aiworkstation-open-source-intelligence.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/zxhwolfe-dev/aiworkstation-open-source-intelligence>) — Research open-source AI projects: search a live radar, compare and shortlist projects, and plan stacks, keeping facts, recommendations and risks separate.

<a id="list-category-47"></a>

### end to end RAG platforms

- [andyliszewski/grounding-ai](../wiki/andyliszewski-grounding-ai.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/andyliszewski/grounding-ai>) — Local searchable index of PDFs, EPUBs and Word documents for grounded answers with page and section citations.
- [gogabrielordonez/mcp-ragchat](../wiki/gogabrielordonez-mcp-ragchat.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/gogabrielordonez/mcp-ragchat>) — Add RAG-based AI chat to any website: local vector store, OpenAI, Anthropic or Gemini models, a self-contained chat server and an embeddable widget.

- [poll-the-people/customgpt-mcp](../wiki/poll-the-people-customgpt-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/Poll-The-People/customgpt-mcp>) — Access the CustomGPT.ai RAG-as-a-service API.
- [vectara/vectara-mcp](../wiki/vectara-vectara-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/vectara/vectara-mcp>) — Access Vectara's RAG-as-a-service platform.
- [zaharajabeen13-create/ai-rete-rag-mcp](../wiki/zaharajabeen13-create-ai-rete-rag-mcp.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/zaharajabeen13-create/ai-rete-rag-mcp>) — Rule-based decisions: a Rete engine returns verdicts explained via retrieval over your policy documents, with rules drafted from policy text and saved as YAML.
